## 任务：修改 run_pipeline.py 添加解码参数 CLI + 生成 exp-003 启动脚本

### 目标
1. 修改 run_pipeline.py，将 generate_depth_chain 中硬编码的解码参数（top_p, temperature, do_sample）改为 CLI 可配置
2. 生成 exp-003 解码策略消融实验的 4 个启动脚本
3. 部署到 autodl-4090d 并 dry-run 验证

### 服务器信息
- 主机: autodl-4090d (已在 ~/.ssh/config 配置)
- 环境: conda base (`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`)
- 项目路径: /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination
- 脚本: /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/scripts/run_pipeline.py

### Step 1: 读取当前代码
用 ssh_read_only 读取远程的 run_pipeline.py，重点关注：
- `generate_depth_chain` 函数签名和 `model.generate` 调用（约 L126-178）
- `main` 函数的 argparse 定义（约 L569-633）

### Step 2: 修改代码（用 ssh_execute 远程编辑）

**修改 1: argparse 添加解码参数**
在 main() 的 argparse 部分（`--feat_batch_size` 之后）添加：
```python
parser.add_argument("--top_p", type=float, default=0.95,
                    help="Nucleus sampling top-p (default: 0.95)")
parser.add_argument("--temperature", type=float, default=1.0,
                    help="Sampling temperature (default: 1.0)")
parser.add_argument("--do_sample", action="store_true", default=True,
                    help="Use sampling (default: True)")
parser.add_argument("--no_sample", dest="do_sample", action="store_false",
                    help="Use greedy decoding")
```

**修改 2: generate_depth_chain 函数签名**
将函数签名改为：
```python
def generate_depth_chain(
    model,
    tokenizer,
    data_dir: Path,
    max_depth: int,
    batch_size: int,
    max_new_tokens: int = 256,
    top_p: float = 0.95,
    temperature: float = 1.0,
    do_sample: bool = True,
):
```

**修改 3: model.generate 调用**
将硬编码值替换为参数：
```python
outputs = model.generate(
    **inputs,
    max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
    do_sample=do_sample,
    top_p=top_p,
    temperature=temperature,
    pad_token_id=tokenizer.pad_token_id,
)
```

**修改 4: main() 调用处**
将 generate_depth_chain 调用改为：
```python
generate_depth_chain(model, tokenizer, data_dir, args.max_depth, args.batch_size,
                     top_p=args.top_p, temperature=args.temperature, do_sample=args.do_sample)
```

**修改 5: 日志输出**
在 main() 的日志部分添加解码参数信息：
```python
log.info(f"Decoding: do_sample={args.do_sample}, top_p={args.top_p}, temperature={args.temperature}")
```

### Step 3: 生成 exp-003 启动脚本
在服务器 /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/ 下创建 `scripts/run_exp003_ablation.sh`：

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# exp-003: Decoding Strategy Ablation on Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct
# 4 configurations, each saves to separate data/results dirs
# Usage: bash scripts/run_exp003_ablation.sh &lt;config_num>
#   1 = greedy
#   2 = nucleus p=0.9, T=1.0
#   3 = nucleus p=0.95, T=0.7
#   4 = nucleus p=0.95, T=1.2

source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base
source /etc/network_turbo 2>/dev/null
export HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache
export HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1
export REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt
export SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt
export HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com

MODEL=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct
BASE=/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination
SCRIPT=$BASE/scripts/run_pipeline.py
N=5000
D=5

case "$1" in
  1)
    DESC="greedy"
    python $SCRIPT --model_path $MODEL --data_dir $BASE/data_exp003_greedy --results_dir $BASE/results_exp003_greedy --num_samples $N --max_depth $D --no_sample
    ;;
  2)
    DESC="nucleus_p0.9_t1.0"
    python $SCRIPT --model_path $MODEL --data_dir $BASE/data_exp003_nuc09 --results_dir $BASE/results_exp003_nuc09 --num_samples $N --max_depth $D --top_p 0.9 --temperature 1.0
    ;;
  3)
    DESC="nucleus_p0.95_t0.7"
    python $SCRIPT --model_path $MODEL --data_dir $BASE/data_exp003_t07 --results_dir $BASE/results_exp003_t07 --num_samples $N --max_depth $D --top_p 0.95 --temperature 0.7
    ;;
  4)
    DESC="nucleus_p0.95_t1.2"
    python $SCRIPT --model_path $MODEL --data_dir $BASE/data_exp003_t12 --results_dir $BASE/results_exp003_t12 --num_samples $N --max_depth $D --top_p 0.95 --temperature 1.2
    ;;
  *)
    echo "Usage: $0 {1|2|3|4}"
    exit 1
    ;;
esac
echo "Completed: $DESC"
```

### Step 4: Dry-run 验证

  --results_dir /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/results_dryrun_decoding \

  --no_sample

# 验证 greedy 模式（do_sample=False）的输出确定性
```

Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef ngrams(tokens, n):\\n    return [tuple(tokens[i : i + n]) for i in range(len(tokens) - n + 1)]\\n\\n\\ndef simple_bleu(candidate_tokens, reference_tokens, max_n=4):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute sentence-level BLEU up to max_n grams with brevity penalty.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(candidate_tokens) == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    precisions = []\\n    for n in range(1, max_n + 1):\\n        cand_ngrams = ngrams(candidate_tokens, n)\\n        ref_ngrams = ngrams(reference_tokens, n)\\n        if not cand_ngrams:\\n            precisions.append(0.0)\\n            continue\\n        ref_counts = Counter(ref_ngrams)\\n        clipped = 0\\n        cand_counts = Counter(cand_ngrams)\\n        for ng, cnt in cand_counts.items():\\n            clipped += min(cnt, ref_counts.get(ng, 0))\\n        precisions.append(clipped / len(cand_ngrams))\\n    if any(p == 0 for p in precisions):\\n        return 0.0\\n    log_avg = sum(math.log(p) for p in precisions) / max_n\\n    bp = min(1.0, math.exp(1 - len(reference_tokens) / max(len(candidate_tokens), 1)))\\n    return bp * math.exp(log_avg)\\n\\n\\n# simple_bleu(candidate_tokens, reference_tokens, max_n=4):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute sentence-level BLEU up to max_n grams with brevity penalty.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(candidate_tokens) == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    precisions = []\\n    for n in range(1, max_n + 1):\\n        cand_ngrams = ngrams(candidate_tokens, n)\\n        ref_ngrams = ngrams(reference_tokens, n)\\n        if not cand_ngrams:\\n            precisions.append(0.0)\\n            continue\\n        ref_counts = Counter(ref_ngrams)\\n        clipped = 0\\n        cand_counts = Counter(cand_ngrams)\\n        for ng, cnt in cand_counts.items():\\n            clipped += min(cnt, ref_counts.get(ng, 0))\\n        precisions.append(clipped / len(cand_ngrams))\\n    if any(p == 0 for p in precisions):\\n        return 0.0\\n    log_avg = sum(math.log(p) for p in precisions) / max_n\\n    bp = min(1.0, math.exp(1 - len(reference_tokens) / max(len(candidate_tokens), 1)))\\n    return bp * math.exp(log_avg)\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 2: Seed data preparation\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef prepare_seed_data(tokenizer, data_dir: Path, num_samples: int, max_tokens: int = 256):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Download C4 validation, truncate to max_tokens, save as depth_0.jsonl.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    out_path = data_dir / \\\"depth_0.jsonl\\\"\\n    if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= num_samples:\\n        log.info(\\\"depth_0.jsonl already exists with enough samples — skipping\\\")\\n        return\\n\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\n\\n    # Use a single validation shard to avoid slow full-tree listing on proxied networks\\n    hf_endpoint = os.environ.get(\\\"HF_ENDPOINT\\\", \\\"https://huggingface.co\\\")\\n    C4_SHARD_URL = f\\\"{hf_endpoint}/datasets/allenai/c4/resolve/main/en/c4-validation.00000-of-00008.json.gz\\\"\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading C4 validation shard (streaming) ...\\\")\\n    try:\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"json\\\", data_files=C4_SHARD_URL, split=\\\"train\\\", streaming=True)\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        log.warning(f\\\"Direct shard download failed ({e}), falling back to full dataset listing\\\")\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"allenai/c4\\\", \\\"en\\\", split=\\\"validation\\\", streaming=True)\\n\\n    records = []\\n    for i, example in enumerate(tqdm(ds, total=num_samples, desc=\\\"Sampling C4\\\")):\\n        if i >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n        text = example[\\\"text\\\"].strip()\\n        if not text:\\n            continue\\n        ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:max_tokens]\\n        truncated = tokenizer.decode(ids, skip_special_tokens=True)\\n        records.append({\\\"text\\\": truncated, \\\"doc_id\\\": len(records)})\\n        if len(records) >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n\\n    data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    with open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for r in records:\\n            f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n desc=f\\\"depth {depth}\\\"):\\n            batch = prev_records[start : start + batch_size]\\n            prompts = [REWRITE_PROMPT.format(prev_text=r[\\\"text\\\"]) for r in batch]\\n\\n            inputs = tokenizer(\\n                prompts,\\n                return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\",\\n                padding=True,\\n                truncation=True,\\n                max_length=512,\\n            ).to(model.device)\\n\\n            with torch.no_grad():\\n                outputs = model.generate(\\n                    **inputs,\\n                    max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,\\n                    do_sample=True,\\n                    top_p=0.95,\\n                    temperature=1.0,\\n                    pad_token_id=tokenizer.pad_token_id,\\n                )\\n\\n            for j, out_ids in enumerate(outputs):\\n                prompt_len = inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1]\\n                generated_ids = out_ids[prompt_len:]\\n                text = tokenizer.decode(generated_ids, skip_special_tokens=True).strip()\\n                results.append({\\\"text\\\": text, \\\"doc_id\\\": batch[j][\\\"doc_id\\\"]})\\n\\n            torch.cuda.empty_cache()\\n\\n        with open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n            for r in results:\\n                f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n        log.info(f\\\"Saved {len(results)} docs to {out_path}\\\")\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 4: Feature extraction\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef compute_surprisal_features(model, tokenizer, texts, batch_size=8):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute 9-dim surprisal statistics per text using batched inference.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    all_features = []\\n    for start in tqdm(range(0, len(texts), batch_size), desc=\\\"Surprisal\\\"):\\n        batch_texts = texts[start : start + batch_size]\\n        inputs = tokenizer(\\n            batch_texts,\\n            return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\",\\n            padding=True,\\n            truncation=True,\\n            max_length=512,\\n        ).to(model.device)\\n\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            logits = model(**inputs).logits  # (B, T, V)\\n\\n        log_probs = torch.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)  # (B, T, V)\\n        input_ids = inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"]  # (B, T)\\n\\n        for i in range(len(batch_texts)):\\n            mask = inputs[\\\"attention_mask\\\"][i].bool()\\n            seq_len = mask.sum().item()\\n            if seq_len < 3:\\n                all_features.append([0.0] * 9)\\n                continue\\n\\n            # left-padding: real tokens are right-aligned, use [-seq_len:]\\n            offset = input_ids.shape[1] - seq_len\\n            token_ids = input_ids[i, offset:]\\n            # surprisal of each token given prefix: -log p(x_t | x_{&lt;t})\\n            # shift: logits[t] predicts token[t+1]\\n            token_log_probs = []\\n            for t in range(1, seq_len):\\n                lp = log_probs[i, offset + t - 1, token_ids[t]].item()\\n                token_log_probs.append(-lp)  # surprisal = -log_prob\\n\\n            arr = np.array(token_log_probs)\\n            if len(arr) < 3:\\n                all_features.append([0.0] * 9)\\n                continue\\n\\n            d1 = np.diff(arr)\\n            d2 = np.diff(d1) if len(d1) > 1 else np.array([0.0])\\n\\n            feats = [\\n                float(np.mean(arr)),\\n                float(np.std(arr)),\\n                float(stats.skew(arr)),\\n                float(stats.kurtosis(arr)),\\n                float(np.mean(d1)),\\n                float(np.std(d1)),\\n                float(stats.skew(d1)) if len(d1) >= 3 else 0.0,\\n                float(np.mean(d2)),\\n                float(np.std(d2)),\\n            ]\\n            all_features.append(feats)\\n\\n        torch.cuda.empty_cache()\\n\\n    return all_features\\n\\n\\ndef compute_vocab_diversity(texts, tokenizer, depth_texts_map, depth, sample_bleu=200, bleu_refs=100):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute 3-dim vocabulary diversity features: TTR, hapax ratio, self-BLEU.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.74}}"}

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nMVP Pilot Saturation Study — Complete Pipeline (Steps 2-5)\\n\\nGenerates recursive depth chains from C4 seed data using Qwen2.5-1.5B,\\nextracts statistical features, and runs distinguishability analysis.\\n\\nInput:  C4 validation split (streamed from HuggingFace)\\nOutput: data/depth_{0..K}.jsonl, data/features.csv, results/*.json + summary.txt\\n\\nKey deps: torch, transformers, datasets, scipy, lightgbm, sklearn, tqdm\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport math\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom scipy import stats\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\",\\n    datefmt=\\\"%H:%M:%S\\\",\\n)\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Utility: simple 4-gram BLEU (no nltk dependency)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef ngrams(tokens, n):\\n    return [tuple(tokens[i : i + n]) for i in range(len(tokens) - n + 1)]\\n\\n\\ndef simple_bleu(candidate_tokens, reference_tokens, max_n=4):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute sentence-level BLEU up to max_n grams with brevity penalty.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(candidate_tokens) == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    precisions = []\\n    for n in range(1, max_n + 1):\\n        cand_ngrams = ngrams(candidate_tokens, n)\\n        ref_ngrams = ngrams(reference_tokens, n)\\n        if not cand_ngrams:\\n            precisions.append(0.0)\\n            continue\\n        ref_counts = Counter(ref_ngrams)\\n        clipped = 0\\n        cand_counts = Counter(cand_ngrams)\\n        for ng, cnt in cand_counts.items():\\n            clipped += min(cnt, ref_counts.get(ng, 0))\\n        precisions.append(clipped / len(cand_ngrams))\\n    if any(p == 0 for p in precisions):\\n        return 0.0\\n    log_avg = sum(math.log(p) for p in precisions) / max_n\\n    bp = min(1.0, math.exp(1 - len(reference_tokens) / max(len(candidate_tokens), 1)))\\n    return bp * math.exp(log_avg)\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 2: Seed data preparation\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef prepare_seed_data(tokenizer, data_dir: Path, num_samples: int, max_tokens: int = 256):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Download C4 validation, truncate to max_tokens, save as depth_0.jsonl.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    out_path = data_dir / \\\"depth_0.jsonl\\\"\\n    if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= num_samples:\\n        log.info(\\\"depth_0.jsonl already exists with enough samples — skipping\\\")\\n        return\\n\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\n\\n    # Use a single validation shard to avoid slow full-tree listing on proxied networks\\n    hf_endpoint = os.environ.get(\\\"HF_ENDPOINT\\\", \\\"https://huggingface.co\\\")\\n    C4_SHARD_URL = f\\\"{hf_endpoint}/datasets/allenai/c4/resolve/main/en/c4-validation.00000-of-00008.json.gz\\\"\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading C4 validation shard (streaming) ...\\\")\\n    try:\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"json\\\", data_files=C4_SHARD_URL, split=\\\"train\\\", streaming=True)\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        log.warning(f\\\"Direct shard download failed ({e}), falling back to full dataset listing\\\")\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"allenai/c4\\\", \\\"en\\\", split=\\\"validation\\\", streaming=True)\\n\\n    records = []\\n    for i, example in enumerate(tqdm(ds, total=num_samples, desc=\\\"Sampling C4\\\")):\\n        if i >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n        text = example[\\\"text\\\"].strip()\\n        if not text:\\n            continue\\n        ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:max_tokens]\\n        truncated = tokenizer.decode(ids, skip_special_tokens=True)\\n        records.append({\\\"text\\\": truncated, \\\"doc_id\\\": len(records)})\\n        if len(records) >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n\\n    data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    with open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for r in records:\\n            f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    log.info(f\\\"Saved {len(records)} seed documents to {out_path}\\\")\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 3: Recursive depth chain generation\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\nREWRITE_PROMPT = (\\n    \\\"Below is a passage. Write a new passage of similar length on the same \\\"\\n    \\\"topic and in the same style.\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n    \\\"Original passage:\\\\n{prev_text}\\\\n\\\\nNew passage:\\\\n\\\"\\n)\\n\\n\\ndef load_jsonl(path: Path):\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        return [json.loads(line) for line in f]\\n\\n\\ndef generate_depth_chain(\\n    model,\\n    tokenizer,\\n    data_dir: Path,\\n    max_depth: int,\\n    batch_size: int,\\n    max_new_tokens: int = 256,\\n):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate depths 1..max_depth via prompt-chain rewriting.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for depth in range(1, max_depth + 1):\\n        out_path = data_dir / f\\\"depth_{depth}.jsonl\\\"\\n        prev_path = data_dir / f\\\"depth_{depth - 1}.jsonl\\\"\\n\\n        if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= sum(1 for _ in open(prev_path)):\\n            log.info(f\\\"depth_{depth}.jsonl already complete — skipping\\\")\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.74}}"}

Qwen2.5-1.5B,\\nextracts statistical features, and runs distinguishability analysis.\\n\\nInput:  C4 validation split (streamed from HuggingFace)\\nOutput: data/depth_{0..K}.jsonl, data/features.csv, results/*.json + summary.txt\\n\\nKey deps: torch, transformers, datasets, scipy, lightgbm, sklearn, tqdm\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport math\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom scipy import stats\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\",\\n    datefmt=\\\"%H:%M:%S\\\",\\n)\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Utility: simple 4-gram BLEU (no nltk dependency)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef ngrams(tokens, n):\\n    return [tuple(tokens[i : i + n]) for i in range(len(tokens) - n + 1)]\\n\\n\\ndef simple_bleu(candidate_tokens, reference_tokens, max_n=4):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute sentence-level BLEU up to max_n grams with brevity penalty.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(candidate_tokens) == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    precisions = []\\n    for n in range(1, max_n + 1):\\n        cand_ngrams = ngrams(candidate_tokens, n)\\n        ref_ngrams = ngrams(reference_tokens, n)\\n        if not cand_ngrams:\\n            precisions.append(0.0)\\n            continue\\n        ref_counts = Counter(ref_ngrams)\\n        clipped = 0\\n        cand_counts = Counter(cand_ngrams)\\n        for ng, cnt in cand_counts.items():\\n            clipped += min(cnt, ref_counts.get(ng, 0))\\n        precisions.append(clipped / len(cand_ngrams))\\n    if any(p == 0 for p in precisions):\\n        return 0.0\\n    log_avg = sum(math.log(p) for p in precisions) / max_n\\n    bp = min(1.0, math.exp(1 - len(reference_tokens) / max(len(candidate_tokens), 1)))\\n    return bp * math.exp(log_avg)\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 2: Seed data preparation\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef prepare_seed_data(tokenizer, data_dir: Path, num_samples: int, max_tokens: int = 256):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Download C4 validation, truncate to max_tokens, save as depth_0.jsonl.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    out_path = data_dir / \\\"depth_0.jsonl\\\"\\n    if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= num_samples:\\n        log.info(\\\"depth_0.jsonl already exists with enough samples — skipping\\\")\\n        return\\n\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\n\\n    # Use a single validation shard to avoid slow full-tree listing on proxied networks\\n    hf_endpoint = os.environ.get(\\\"HF_ENDPOINT\\\", \\\"https://huggingface.co\\\")\\n    C4_SHARD_URL = f\\\"{hf_endpoint}/datasets/allenai/c4/resolve/main/en/c4-validation.00000-of-00008.json.gz\\\"\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading C4 validation shard (streaming) ...\\\")\\n    try:\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"json\\\", data_files=C4_SHARD_URL, split=\\\"train\\\", streaming=True)\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        log.warning(f\\\"Direct shard download failed ({e}), falling back to full dataset listing\\\")\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"allenai/c4\\\", \\\"en\\\", split=\\\"validation\\\", streaming=True)\\n\\n    records = []\\n    for i, example in enumerate(tqdm(ds, total=num_samples, desc=\\\"Sampling C4\\\")):\\n        if i >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n        text = example[\\\"text\\\"].strip()\\n        if not text:\\n            continue\\n        ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:max_tokens]\\n        truncated = tokenizer.decode(ids, skip_special_tokens=True)\\n        records.append({\\\"text\\\": truncated, \\\"doc_id\\\": len(records)})\\n        if len(records) >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n\\n    data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    with open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for r in records:\\n            f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    log.info(f\\\"Saved {len(records)} seed documents to {out_path}\\\")\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 3: Recursive depth chain generation\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\nREWRITE_PROMPT = (\\n    \\\"Below is a passage. Write a new passage of similar length on the same \\\"\\n    \\\"topic and in the same style.\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n    \\\"Original passage:\\\\n{prev_text}\\\\n\\\\nNew passage:\\\\n\\\"\\n)\\n\\n\\ndef load_jsonl(path: Path):\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        return [json.loads(line) for line in f]\\n\\n\\ndef generate_depth_chain(\\n    model,\\n    tokenizer,\\n    data_dir: Path,\\n    max_depth: int,\\n    batch_size: int,\\n    max_new_tokens: int = 256,\\n):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate depths 1..max_depth via prompt-chain rewriting.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for depth in range(1, max_depth + 1):\\n        out_path = data_dir / f\\\"depth_{depth}.jsonl\\\"\\n        prev_path = data_dir / f\\\"depth_{depth - 1}.jsonl\\\"\\n\\n        if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= sum(1 for _ in open(prev_path)):\\n            log.info(f\\\"depth_{depth}.jsonl already complete — skipping\\\")\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.74}}"

Qwen2.5-1.5B,\\nextracts statistical features, and runs distinguishability analysis.\\n\\nInput:  C4 validation split (streamed from HuggingFace)\\nOutput: data/depth_{0..K}.jsonl, data/features.csv, results/*.json + summary.txt\\n\\nKey deps: torch, transformers, datasets, scipy, lightgbm, sklearn, tqdm\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport math\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom scipy import stats\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\",\\n    datefmt=\\\"%H:%M:%S\\\",\\n)\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Utility: simple 4-gram BLEU (no nltk dependency)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef ngrams(tokens, n):\\n    return [tuple(tokens[i : i + n]) for i in range(len(tokens) - n + 1)]\\n\\n\\ndef simple_bleu(candidate_tokens, reference_tokens, max_n=4):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute sentence-level BLEU up to max_n grams with brevity penalty.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(candidate_tokens) == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    precisions = []\\n    for n in range(1, max_n + 1):\\n        cand_ngrams = ngrams(candidate_tokens, n)\\n        ref_ngrams = ngrams(reference_tokens, n)\\n        if not cand_ngrams:\\n            precisions.append(0.0)\\n            continue\\n        ref_counts = Counter(ref_ngrams)\\n        clipped = 0\\n        cand_counts = Counter(cand_ngrams)\\n        for ng, cnt in cand_counts.items():\\n            clipped += min(cnt, ref_counts.get(ng, 0))\\n        precisions.append(clipped / len(cand_ngrams))\\n    if any(p == 0 for p in precisions):\\n        return 0.0\\n    log_avg = sum(math.log(p) for p in precisions) / max_n\\n    bp = min(1.0, math.exp(1 - len(reference_tokens) / max(len(candidate_tokens), 1)))\\n    return bp * math.exp(log_avg)\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 2: Seed data preparation\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef prepare_seed_data(tokenizer, data_dir: Path, num_samples: int, max_tokens: int = 256):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Download C4 validation, truncate to max_tokens, save as depth_0.jsonl.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    out_path = data_dir / \\\"depth_0.jsonl\\\"\\n    if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= num_samples:\\n        log.info(\\\"depth_0.jsonl already exists with enough samples — skipping\\\")\\n        return\\n\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\n\\n    # Use a single validation shard to avoid slow full-tree listing on proxied networks\\n    hf_endpoint = os.environ.get(\\\"HF_ENDPOINT\\\", \\\"https://huggingface.co\\\")\\n    C4_SHARD_URL = f\\\"{hf_endpoint}/datasets/allenai/c4/resolve/main/en/c4-validation.00000-of-00008.json.gz\\\"\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading C4 validation shard (streaming) ...\\\")\\n    try:\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"json\\\", data_files=C4_SHARD_URL, split=\\\"train\\\", streaming=True)\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        log.warning(f\\\"Direct shard download failed ({e}), falling back to full dataset listing\\\")\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"allenai/c4\\\", \\\"en\\\", split=\\\"validation\\\", streaming=True)\\n\\n    records = []\\n    for i, example in enumerate(tqdm(ds, total=num_samples, desc=\\\"Sampling C4\\\")):\\n        if i >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n        text = example[\\\"text\\\"].strip()\\n        if not text:\\n            continue\\n        ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:max_tokens]\\n        truncated = tokenizer.decode(ids, {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nMVP Pilot Saturation Study — Complete Pipeline (Steps 2-5)\\n\\nGenerates recursive depth chains from C4 seed data using Qwen2.5-1.5B,\\nextracts statistical features, and runs distinguishability analysis.\\n\\nInput:  C4 validation split (streamed from HuggingFace)\\nOutput: data/depth_{0..K}.jsonl, data/features.csv, results/*.json + summary.txt\\n\\nKey deps: torch, transformers, datasets, scipy, lightgbm, sklearn, tqdm\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport math\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom scipy import stats\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\",\\n    datefmt=\\\"%H:%M:%S\\\",\\n)\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Utility: simple 4-gram BLEU (no nltk dependency)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef ngrams(tokens, n):\\n    return [tuple(tokens[i : i + n]) for i in range(len(tokens) - n + 1)]\\n\\n\\ndef simple_bleu(candidate_tokens, reference_tokens, max_n=4):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute sentence-level BLEU up to max_n grams with brevity penalty.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(candidate_tokens) == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    precisions = []\\n    for n in range(1, max_n + 1):\\n        cand_ngrams = ngrams(candidate_tokens, n)\\n        ref_ngrams = ngrams(reference_tokens, n)\\n        if not cand_ngrams:\\n            precisions.append(0.0)\\n            continue\\n        ref_counts = Counter(ref_ngrams)\\n        clipped = 0\\n        cand_counts = Counter(cand_ngrams)\\n        for ng, cnt in cand_counts.items():\\n            clipped += min(cnt, ref_counts.get(ng, 0))\\n        precisions.append(clipped / len(cand_ngrams))\\n    if any(p == 0 for p in precisions):\\n        return 0.0\\n    log_avg = sum(math.log(p) for p in precisions) / max_n\\n    bp = min(1.0, math.exp(1 - len(reference_tokens) / max(len(candidate_tokens), 1)))\\n    return bp * math.exp(log_avg)\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 2: Seed data preparation\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef prepare_seed_data(tokenizer, data_dir: Path, num_samples: int, max_tokens: int = 256):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Download C4 validation, truncate to max_tokens, save as depth_0.jsonl.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    out_path = data_dir / \\\"depth_0.jsonl\\\"\\n    if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= num_samples:\\n        log.info(\\\"depth_0.jsonl already exists with enough samples — skipping\\\")\\n        return\\n\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\n\\n    # Use a single validation shard to avoid slow full-tree listing on proxied networks\\n    hf_endpoint = os.environ.get(\\\"HF_ENDPOINT\\\", \\\"https://huggingface.co\\\")\\n    C4_SHARD_URL = f\\\"{hf_endpoint}/datasets/allenai/c4/resolve/main/en/c4-validation.00000-of-00008.json.gz\\\"\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading C4 validation shard (streaming) ...\\\")\\n    try:\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"json\\\", data_files=C4_SHARD_URL, split=\\\"train\\\", streaming=True)\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        log.warning(f\\\"Direct shard download failed ({e}), falling back to full dataset listing\\\")\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"allenai/c4\\\", \\\"en\\\", split=\\\"validation\\\", streaming=True)\\n\\n    records = []\\n    for i, example in enumerate(tqdm(ds, total=num_samples, desc=\\\"Sampling C4\\\")):\\n        if i >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n        text = example[\\\"text\\\"].strip()\\n        if not text:\\n            continue\\n        ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:max_tokens]\\n        truncated = tokenizer.decode(ids, skip_special_tokens=True)\\n        records.append({\\\"text\\\": truncated, \\\"doc_id\\\": len(records)})\\n        if len(records) >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n\\n    data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    with open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for r in records:\\n            f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    log.info(f\\\"Saved {len(records)} seed documents to {out_path}\\\")\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 3: Recursive depth chain generation\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\nREWRITE_PROMPT = (\\n    \\\"Below is a passage. Write a new passage of similar length on the same \\\"\\n    \\\"topic and in the same style.\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n    \\\"Original passage:\\\\n{prev_text}\\\\n\\\\nNew passage:\\\\n\\\"\\n)\\n\\n\\ndef load_jsonl(path: Path):\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        return [json.loads(line) for line in f]\\n\\n\\ndef generate_depth_chain(\\n    model,\\n    tokenizer,\\n    data_dir: Path,\\n    max_depth: int,\\n    batch_size: int,\\n    max_new_tokens: int = 256,\\n    top_p: float = 0.95,\\n    temperature: float = 1.0,\\n    do_sample: bool = True,\\n):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate depths 1..max_depth via prompt-chain rewriting.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for depth in range(1, max_depth + 1):\\n        out_path = data_dir / f\\\"depth_{depth}.jsonl\\\"\\n        prev_path = data_dir / f\\\"depth_{depth - 1}.jsonl\\\"\\n\\n        if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= sum(1 for _ in open(prev_path)):\\n            log.info(f\\\"depth_{depth}.jsonl already complete — skipping\\\")\\n            continue\\n\\n        prev_records = load_jsonl(prev_path)\\n        log.info(f\\\"Generating depth {depth} ({len(prev_records)} docs, batch_size={batch_size}) ...\\\")\\n\\n        results = []\\n        for start in tqdm(range(0, len(prev_records), batch_size), desc=f\\\"depth {depth}\\\"):\\n            batch = prev_records[start : start + batch_size]\\n            prompts = [REWRITE_PROMPT.format(prev_text=r[\\\"text\\\"]) for r in batch]\\n\\n            inputs = tokenizer(\\n                prompts,\\n                return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\",\\n                padding=True,\\n                truncation=True,\\n                max_length=512,\\n            ).to(model.device)\\n\\n            with torch.no_grad():\\n                outputs = model.generate(\\n                    **inputs,\\n                    max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,\\n                    do_sample=do_sample,\\n                    top_p=top_p,\\n                    temperature=temperature,\\n                    pad_token_id=tokenizer.pad_token_id,\\n                )\\n\\n            for j, out_ids in enumerate(outputs):\\n                prompt_len = inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].shape[1]\\n                generated_ids = out_ids[prompt_len:]\\n                text = tokenizer.decode(generated_ids, skip_special_tokens=True).strip()\\n                results.append({\\\"text\\\": text, \\\"doc_id\\\": batch[j][\\\"doc_id\\\"]})\\n\\n            torch.cuda.empty_cache()\\n\\n        with open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n            for r in results:\\n                f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n        log.info(f\\\"Saved {len(results)} docs to {out_path}\\\")\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 4: Feature extraction\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef compute_surprisal_features(model, tokenizer, texts, batch_size=8):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute 9-dim surprisal statistics per text using batched inference.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    all_features = []\\n    for start in tqdm(range(0, len(texts), batch_size), desc=\\\"Surprisal\\\"):\\n        batch_texts = texts[start : start + batch_size]\\n        inputs = tokenizer(\\n            batch_texts,\\n 125	
126	def generate_depth_chain(
127	    model,
128	    tokenizer,
129	    data_dir: Path,
130	    max_depth: int,
131	    batch_size: int,
132	    max_new_tokens: int = 256,
133	):
134	    """Generate depths 1..max_depth via prompt-chain rewriting."""
135	    for depth in range(1, max_depth + 1):
136	        out_path = data_dir / f"depth_{depth}.jsonl"
137	        prev_path = data_dir / f"depth_{depth - 1}.jsonl"
138	
139	        if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= sum(1 for _ in open(prev_path)):
140	            log.info(f"depth_{depth}.jsonl already complete — skipping")
141	            continue
142	
143	        prev_records = load_jsonl(prev_path)
144	        log.info(f"Generating depth {depth} ({len(prev_records)} docs, batch_size={batch_size}) ...")
145	
146	        results = []
147	        for start in tqdm(range(0, len(prev_records), batch_size), desc=f"depth {depth}"):
148	            batch = prev_records[start : start + batch_size]
149	            prompts = [REWRITE_PROMPT.format(prev_text=r["text"]) for r in batch]
150	
151	            inputs = tokenizer(
152	                prompts,
153	                return_tensors="pt",
154	                padding=True,
155	                truncation=True,
156	                max_length=512,
157	            ).to(model.device)
158	
159	            with torch.no_grad():
160	                outputs = model.generate(
161	                    **inputs,
162	                    max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
163	                    do_sample=True,
164	                    top_p=0.95,
165	                    temperature=1.0,
166	                    pad_token_id=tokenizer.pad_token_id,
167	                )
168	
169	            for j, out_ids in enumerate(outputs):
170	                prompt_len = inputs["input_ids"].shape[1]
171	                generated_ids = out_ids[prompt_len:]
172	                text = tokenizer.decode(generated_ids, skip_special_tokens=True).strip()
173	                results.append({"text": text, "doc_id": batch[j]["doc_id"]})
174

      "content": "\ndef generate_depth_chain(\n    model,\n    tokenizer,\n    data_dir: Path,\n    max_depth: int,\n    batch_size: int,\n    max_new_tokens: int = 256,\n):\n    \"\"\"Generate depths 1..max_depth via prompt-chain rewriting.\"\"\"\n    for depth in range(1, max_depth + 1):\n        out_path = data_dir / f\"depth_{depth}.jsonl\"\n        prev_path = data_dir / f\"depth_{depth - 1}.jsonl\"\n\n        if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= sum(1 for _ in open(prev_path)):\n            log.info(f\"depth_{depth}.jsonl already complete — skipping\")\n            continue\n\n        prev_records = load_jsonl(prev_path)\n        log.info(f\"Generating depth {depth} ({len(prev_records)} docs, batch_size={batch_size}) ...\")\n\n        results = []\n        for start in tqdm(range(0, len(prev_records), batch_size), desc=f\"depth {depth}\"):\n            batch = prev_records[start : start + batch_size]\n            prompts = [REWRITE_PROMPT.format(prev_text=r[\"text\"]) for r in batch]\n\n            inputs = tokenizer(\n                prompts,\n                return_tensors=\"pt\",\n                padding=True,\n                truncation=True,\n                max_length=512,\n            ).to(model.device)\n\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                outputs = model.generate(\n                    **inputs,\n                    max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,\n                    do_sample=True,\n                    top_p=0.95,\n                    temperature=1.0,\n                    pad_token_id=tokenizer.pad_token_id,\n                )\n\n            for j, out_ids in enumerate(outputs):\n                prompt_len = inputs[\"input_ids\"].shape[1]\n                generated_ids = out_ids[prompt_len:]\n                text = tokenizer.decode(generated_ids, skip_special_tokens=True).strip()\n                results.append({\"text\": text, \"doc_id\": batch[j][\"doc_id\"]})\n",

  "content": "#!/bin/bash\n# exp-003: Decoding Strategy Ablation on Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct\n# 4 configurations, each saves to separate data/results dirs\n# Usage: bash scripts/run_exp003_ablation.sh &lt;config_num>\n#   1 = greedy\n#   2 = nucleus p=0.9, T=1.0\n#   3 = nucleus p=0.95, T=0.7\n#   4 = nucleus p=0.95, T=1.2\n\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\nsource /etc/network_turbo 2>/dev/null\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\nexport HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com\n\nMODEL=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct\nBASE=/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination\nSCRIPT=$BASE/scripts/run_pipeline.py\nN=5000\nD=5\n\ncase \"$1\" in\n  1)\n    DESC=\"greedy\"\n    python $SCRIPT --model_path $MODEL --data_dir $BASE/data_exp003_greedy --results_dir $BASE/results_exp003_greedy --num_samples $N --max_depth $D --no_sample\n    ;;\n  2)\n    DESC=\"nucleus_p0.9_t1.0\"\n    python $SCRIPT --model_path $MODEL --data_dir $BASE/data_exp003_nuc09 --results_dir $BASE/results_exp003_nuc09 --num_samples $N --max_depth $D --top_p 0.9 --temperature 1.0\n    ;;\n  3)\n    DESC=\"nucleus_p0.95_t0.7\"\n    python $SCRIPT --model_path $MODEL --data_dir $BASE/data_exp003_t07 --results_dir $BASE/results_exp003_t07 --num_samples $N --max_depth $D --top_p 0.95 --temperature 0.7\n    ;;\n  4)\n    DESC=\"nucleus_p0.95_t1.2\"\n    python $SCRIPT --model_path $MODEL --data_dir $BASE/data_exp003_t12 --results_dir $BASE/results_exp003_t12 --num_samples $N --max_depth $D --top_p 0.95 --temperature 1.2\n    ;;\n  *)\n    echo \"Usage: $0 {1|2|3|4}\"\n    exit 1\n    ;;\nesac\necho \"Completed: $DESC\"\n"
}
